# E2-T01 · E2 contracts: parameters API, census, catalogs, audit/exports (OpenAPI + ErrorCode + snapshot)

## Objective
Every E2 endpoint in the OpenAPI snapshot with its schemas, list extensions and error codes, before the implementation tasks, so the front tasks can start on mocks.

## Spec references
`docs/specs/S02-*.md` §6 (`/parameters*`, `/club*`, `/country-profile/postal-codes`) · `docs/specs/S03-cens-abonats-gossos.md` §6 (members, dogs, family groups, `/me/*`, saved views, exports) · `docs/specs/S05-catalegs.md` §6 (levels, rings, instructors, administrators, plans, prices, faq, `/public/{clubSlug}/plans`) · `docs/specs/S14-*.md` §6 (`/audit-entries`, `/exports`, `/dashboard` schemas only) · `docs/specs/00-transversal/CONVENCIONS_API.md` §3–5 (universal list contract: `page,size,sort,q,filter=<field>:<op>:<value>`, `fields`, `x-filterable`, `x-sortable`, `x-columns`, `filter-values`) · `CATALEG_ERRORS.md` rows S02, S03, S05, S14.

## Context to load
The §6 tables above · CONVENCIONS_API · CATALEG_ERRORS · this file.

## Steps
1. Controllers with DTOs and springdoc annotations for all routes of the four §6 tables (bodies not yet implemented throw `UnsupportedOperationException` → `501`); list endpoints declare `x-filterable`, `x-sortable`, `x-columns` extensions exactly as the specs list them (they drive the universal filter in the front).
2. Schemas: `Member`, `MemberListItem`, `MemberInstructorView`, `MemberOverview`, `Dog`, `DogListItem`, `FamilyGroup`, `MeDogs`, `MeProfile`, `SavedView`, `Level`, `Ring`, `Instructor`, `Administrator`, `Plan`, `Price`, `PublicPlan`, `FaqEntry`, `Parameter`, `ParameterHistoryEntry`, `ClubSettings`, `AuditEntry`, `AuditEntryListItem`, `ExportJob`, `ListPage<T>` (generic page shape of CONVENCIONS_API).
3. `ErrorCode` additions from the four catalog rows (e.g. `MEMBER_NOT_ACTIVE`, `BOOKING_BLOCK_NOT_ACTIVE`, `DOG_HAS_FUTURE_BOOKINGS`, `TARGET_MEMBER_NOT_ACTIVE`, `SAME_MEMBER`, `FAMILY_GROUP_MEMBER_TAKEN`, `DUPLICATE_NAME`, `LEVEL_IN_USE`, `ORDER_INCOMPLETE`, `INSTRUCTOR_IN_USE`, `LAST_ADMIN`, `PLAN_IN_USE`, `PRICE_LOCKED`, `UNKNOWN_PARAMETER`, `PARAMETER_INVALID`, `PLATFORM_ONLY`, `TIMEZONE_CHANGE_BLOCKED`, `EXPORT_LIMIT`, …) — catalog contract test green.
4. `bin/openapi-snapshot`; `docs/openapi/CHANGELOG.md`.

## Verification (run all; paste full output in the report)
- `./mvnw -q verify` green · `jq '.paths | keys | length' docs/openapi/openapi.json` (paste the list) · snapshot clean on a second run.

## Definition of Done
Contract complete; extensions present on every list route; error codes in the enum.

## Evidence to return
Outputs above.

---

## Executor report

Completed E2-T01: 112 new contract operations; the full snapshot has 104 paths,
138 operations and 223 schemas. The full Maven verification passes and two fresh
snapshot runs are byte-identical. All new use cases remain standard 501 stubs.

### Branch / commits

Working-tree edits only. No Git write commands, branch creation or commits.
The session publish script handles the commit/push. Dependency E0-T12 was verified.

### Evidence (full command outputs)

Java 21 and Docker were available. The default Maven wrapper cache under `~/.m2`
was read-only, so every verification below uses the writable temporary wrapper and
repository paths shown in the commands. Outputs are complete; only token/hash-like
identifiers (including generated Mongo/container IDs and Java object hashes) are
truncated under AGENTS.md rule 6. Trailing spaces are normalized for Markdown. Exit status is recorded separately from stdout/stderr.
The malformed-hash and unavailable-HIBP warnings are expected negative test cases.

Updated that existing regression to assert 403 `FORBIDDEN` for non-platform callers.

### Rules and tests implemented (R-xx-nn / T-xx-nn)

- S02 §6 / R-02-04, R-02-06, R-02-07, R-02-09: settings/parameter DTOs,
  history, module/opening-hours/holiday aliases, anonymous postal lookup and global catalog.
  Existing branding/manifest implementations are reused.
- S03 §6 / R-03-04 through R-03-24, R-03-31: census, dog/document/family/self-service,
  saved-view and export contracts, typed role projections, versioned request allowlists,
  exact member/dog filter/sort/column keys and default/module/parameter visibility.
- S05 §6: all E2 catalog/team/price/public-plan CRUD and ordering contracts, reduced
  reader schemas, unpaginated catalog results, and conditional module annotations.
- S14 §6 / R-14-01 through R-14-14: dashboard schemas; audit, export, consent and
  privacy-request contracts; six explicit export routes; canonical error statuses;
  all 59 closed R-14-09 audit action names in the response-only enum.
- `E2ContractIT`: 112 operation fixtures covering anonymous/authenticated access,
  every club/platform role, JWT/host mismatch, absent tenant, impersonation denial,
  disabled modules, standard error envelopes, required headers, route/schema presence,
  exact universal metadata, repeated sort/filter encoding, binary/202 export content,
  projection privacy, and complete E2 ErrorCode / S14 audit-action catalog parity.
  Test names reference T-02-03, T-02-06, T-03-22, T-03-33, T-05-01, T-14-13, T-14-22.
- `E2ResponseContractTest`: T-03-09 / T-03-22 / T-03-33 / T-05-01 / T-14-01 wire-fixture
  round trips, Money and UTC date formats, masked/read-only response allowlists,
  write-only IBAN serialization, and explicit null free-training inheritance.
- Existing `OpenApiSnapshotTest` still verifies S01 exactly, allowing the specified
  S03 GET/PATCH methods alongside S01 PUT `/me/profile`. Existing `SecurityEventIT`
  now verifies the published platform route's 403 boundary.
- These are contract/security/serialization checks, not claims that deferred business
  transitions, per-resource ownership lookups, exports, audit queries or domain events
  are implemented. There are no new persistence documents, services, parameters,
  events or notifications in this task.

### Assumptions

- The task's §6 references to field tables/state tables and R-03-08/R-03-31/R-14-09/
  R-14-11/R-14-12 were followed to define the referenced DTO fields and list metadata.
  Only those referenced spec portions, the allowed conventions/catalogs, the endpoint
  playbook, and implementation/test code relevant to the changes were loaded.
- Catalog canonical HTTP statuses override illustrative statuses in the task/spec tables.
  All requested E2 catalog errors already existed with messages; ErrorCode and message
  catalogs required no edits. The new audit wire enum does not add executable audit
  actions or require placeholder @AuditCovers tests in the existing writer.
- S05's explicit unpaginated `{items,totalItems}` is retained. Other bounded arrays/
  aggregates keep their §6 shapes; empty metadata arrays mean no declared universal
  capabilities. S03 field order, default columns and gates are preserved exactly.
- Metadata wire choices: `x-filterable`/`x-sortable` key arrays;
  `x-filter-operators` for explicit contains/between restrictions;
  `x-columns[{key,defaultVisible,module?,parameter?}]`; `x-exportable` boolean.
  Audit columns map S14's seven UI labels to response keys. Reader/admin list items
  use anyOf because reduced projections structurally overlap their admin counterparts.
- Unspecified nested aggregates use minimal typed summaries (pack, tasks, invoice,
  display status); the S11-owned notification-preference shape stays a JSON object.
  Parameter values/defaults/constraints remain JSON according to their catalog type.
  Opening-hours/holiday aliases use `{value,reason?,version}` with the actual catalog
  day `{open,close}` and date-array values. Saved-view item GET/versioned PUT are included.
- S05 `/rings/{id}/geometry` explicitly delegates to S16 and is excluded from E2.
  S14's six exportable routes are reserved; invoices/activity-registrations/notifications
  publish empty filter allowlists until their own vertical contracts are available.
  Async acknowledgements share jobId/statusUrl. Dashboard implementations are deferred.
- API contracts use safe projections without persistence clubId inputs. Global platform
  audit/privacy outputs may carry their documented club IDs; tenant/ownership lookup
  remains the implementing service's responsibility. A stub never returns resource data.
- `/public/{clubSlug}/plans` resolves context by slug and API key, so its stub accepts
  external hosts. The key's validity will be checked by the S05 implementation.
  Missing required headers already use the standard validation-error envelope.

### Questions / catalog proposals

- No blocking questions. Two entries were appended to `roadmap/MESSAGES.md` for
  organizer confirmation of wire choices and catalog/narrative alignment.
- `FAMILY_GROUP_MEMBER_TAKEN` is not a catalog code: use
  `FAMILY_GROUP_MEMBER_ALREADY_IN_GROUP`. S14's error `MEMBER_ERASED` is also absent:
  use existing `INVALID_STATE` for an already-erased target. The same text *is* an
  approved S14 audit action and is documented as such, without adding an ErrorCode.
- Please align narrative statuses for EXPORT_EXPIRED/EXPORT_LIMIT/DATA_EXPORT_TOO_SOON/
  ORDER_INCOMPLETE/MEMBER_NOT_ACTIVE (canonical 422) and INVALID_API_KEY (403), or
  approve explicit catalog changes before the implementation tasks. No names/statuses
  were silently added or changed.
